Let's think of a direct stochastic algorithm: Start from central. While time, generate perturbations of p:
- Choose a random low-frequency curve m_i (e.g., sinusoid) and update p_i <- p_i + ε m_i, clip, normalize. Evaluate.

- Choose random-smooth perturbation (FFT random phases, low-pass) and add.

    n=len(p); M=max(autocorr(p))
    # Build A rows for constraints f*g <= M. Use normalized p sum1.
    # To save time, use all rows? For n=512, 1023x512 dense 0.5M, okay.
    # But linprog with highs may be several seconds. Use only rows with some heuristic? Use all to be safe.
    # Vectorize A: for each i, f shifted? A[k,i]=p[k-i] if 0<=k-i<n else 0.
    A = np.zeros((2n-1,n))
    for i in range(n):
        val = p[i::-1]? Row k: i from max(0,k-n+1)..min(k,n-1): A[k,i]=p[k-i]
    # Matrix A is block Toeplitz. Use scipy.linalg.toeplitz? For linear conv, rows are prefix/suffix. Could build using np.lib.stride_tricks? Simpler loop O(n²).
    objective = -np.ones(n)
    bounds = [(0,None)]*n + ?
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2n-1,M), bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    g=res.x; if sum>0: g/=sum(g); return g
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-3
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # initial best central 1024 exact, height_seq
    best = central(1024); best_score=score_exact(best)
    if height_sequence_1: best=...
    # Phase A: latent DE at n=512 if time > 120s
    n_de = 256 if budget small else 512
    if deadline - t0 > 90:
       shape_params = random latent initial near central and bounds
       run differential_evolution(obj, bounds, seed=seed, maxiter=80, popsize=18, tol=1e-6, init=['latinhypercube'], polish=False, update_interval=0)
       p_de=softmax(logit(params))
       full_lbfgs(p_de, n_de, budget=min(60, remaining*0.3))
       if score<best: interpolate to 1024
    # Phase B: direct multi-start full L-BFGS at n=512 or 1024? Maybe 512.
       starts: p_de, central, height interpolated, random latent few, block/two-step
       for top starts run full_lbfgs with budget each 10-20s
    # Phase C: if best n<1024, interpolate to 1024; run full_lbfgs at 1024 budget 30s
    # Phase D: SOTA LP on best if n<=512 and time >60s, blend and polish
    # Phase E: final random smooth hill-climb for remaining
    # final exact validation, return list.
```

Full L-BFGS implementation details:
```
def smooth_score_grad_y(y, beta):
   n=y.size+1
   terms = np.empty(n); terms[0]=0; terms[1:]=y
   terms = np.clip(terms, -700, 700)
   terms -= np.max(terms)
   p = np.exp(terms); p /= p.sum()
   c = fft_autocorr(p)  # or direct if n<128
   M = c.max()
   if beta... z=beta*(c-M); z=np.clip(z,-700,0); ez=exp(z); S=sum(ez); L=M+log(S)/beta
   w=ez/S
   # compute h_i as conv p, w_rev
   if n small direct? use np.convolve(p, w[::-1]) length 3n-2
   h = conv[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]
   gp=2*h
   Gsum=np.dot(p,gp)
   grad_y = gp[1:] - p[1:]*Gsum
   val=2*n*L
   return val, grad_y
```
Need check gradient formula equals derivative of β*L? We define L = (1/β)log∑e^{βc} (smooth max). In smooth_obj we can return β? We can simply set `merit = 2n * L`. Then gradient =2n/β * ∂logsum/∂y. Our gp currently ∂logsum/∂p? Actually ∂L/∂p = w? Let's derive:
L = (1/β) log∑e^{βc_k}. ∂L/∂p_i = ∑_k w_k ∂c_k/∂p_i = ∑ w_k 2p_{k-i} = 2h_i = gp. Good, no 1/β factor because weights w=βe^{βc}/∑? Wait define w_k = e^{β(c-M)}/Z; then ∂L/∂p_i = ∑ w_k 2p_{k-i}. Yes gp as 2h with h using w normalized. Correct. ∂L/∂y_j = g_j - p_j∑p_i g_i. Thus grad_y = p? Wait formula: ∂c/∂y_j = ∂c/∂p_i (p_i δ_ij - p_i p_j). Sum_i g_i p_i(δ_ij-p_j)=g_j - p_j G. Yes no extra p_j? It's g_j - p_j G. So `grad_y = gp[1:] - p[1:]*G`. Good. (Not p_j times? yes g_j - p_j G, no p_j factor separately). This is because dp_i = p_i(...). Correct.
Objective val=2nL. Gradient = 2n∂L/∂y. So return grad = 2n*(gp[1:]-p[1:]*G). In previous code I had G = C*(dL - 2L a/s²) for unanchored; okay.

Check softmax derivative for fixed y0: p_i all include p0; G=∑p_i g_i. For j>0, derivative =g_j - p_j G. Correct.

FFA autocorr: Use np.convolve direct for n<=512, FFT for n>512? direct 1M for 1024; 500 calls=500M, okay maybe 1s? We'll implement FFT for n>=256 to be safe. But FFT may introduce tiny negative; use `.real`.

```
def fft_autocorr(p):
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1; N=1<<(L-1).bit_length()
   f=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(f*np.conj(f),N)[:L]
   # due rounding, tiny max negative? clip
   c = np.where(c>0,c,0) # but if all positive? okay
   return c
```
For small n direct exact: `np.convolve(p,p)`.

DE latent function:
```
def latent_logits(params, n, basis_cache):
    rL, rR, coeffs... = params
    t = basis_cache.t
    logit = -rL*np.log(t+1/n) - rR*np.log(1-t+1/n)
    for coeff, basis in zip(...): logit += coeff*basis
    logit -= logit.mean()
    return logit
```

```
def latent_objective(x, n, basis, logL, logR, t):
   rL=x[0]; rR=x[1]
   logit = -rL*logL - rR*logR
   for idx, b in enumerate(basis): logit += x[2+idx]*b
   logit -= logit.mean()
   # stable softmax
   m=logit.max(); z=np.exp(logit-m); s=z.sum(); if s<=0 return 10; p=z/s
   return score_fast(p)
```
Basis arrays should be centered? We subtract logit mean, which makes basis constants irrelevant. Good.

Simple DE algorithm:
```
def simple_de(obj_func, args, bounds, size, max_evals, seed):
   pop = [rng.uniform(bounds) for _ in range(size)]
   best_x, best_f = sort
   for gen while max_evals and time:
      for i in range(size):
         if time/budget break
         a,b,c distinct
         mut = pop[a] + (F=0.8)*(pop[b]-pop[c])
         mut clipped
         fmut=eval
         compare with pop[i]
   return best
```
This is not as robust as scipy DE but better than random. It allows time. Use 20 pop, max evals maybe 2000. Each eval score. Could be enough. Let's implement our own `de_search_latent(n,basis,...,budget,seed)`:

   Evaluate mutant. If better than individual, replace.
   Keep track best.

def init_y(p):
 p=normalize(p); n=p.size
 if n==1: return empty
 p0 = max(float(p[0]), 1e-300)
 if p0 < 1e-12: p0=1e-12; p=p.copy(); p[0]=p0; p/p.sum()
 y=np.log(p[1:]/p0)
 y=np.clip(y, -45,45)
 return y
```

Now `anneal_polish`:
```
def anneal_polish(p, budget_s, rng):
   p=normalize(p); cur=p.copy(); sc=score_fast(cur); best=p.copy(); best_s=sc
   deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
   step=0
   while time<deadline:
       move_type = rng.integers(0,3)
       if move_type==0:
          # lowband logit
          logp = np.log(cur + 1e-300)
          n=len(cur)
          a=rng.normal(0,0.5); b=rng.normal(0,0.5); c=rng.normal(0,0.5)
          t=np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1)
          logit = a*np.sin(2*np.pi*t)+b*np.cos(4*np.pi*t)+c*np.sin(8*np.pi*t)
          amp = 0.2 + 0.2*rng.random()
          np.add(logp, amp*logit, out=logp)
          cand = np.exp(logp - np.max(logp)); cand/=cand.sum()
       elif move_type==1:
          cand=cur.copy()
          L=max(1,n//20)
          width=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, L+1)))
          start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
          scale=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.35,width))
          cand[start:start+width]*=scale
          cand=normalize(cand)
       else:
          cand=cur.copy()
          # multi block
          for _ in range(int(rng.integers(1,3))):
             width=...; scale=...
          cand=normalize(cand)
       sc2=score_fast(cand)
       if finite and sc2 < sc: update cur
       if sc2 < best_s: update best
       # occasional accept worse? simple T=0.005:
       elif finite and sc2 - sc < 0.005 and rng.random()<0.3:
            cur=cand; sc=sc2
       # no improve: reset? 
       if step%1000: print
   return best
```
Normalize after cand. The lowband step uses `np.add` but logp maybe huge; stable softmax. This may be okay.
For n=1024, each score exact? Use fast. Many moves maybe 10k; okay.

SOTA LP: Let's include but call only if time >50 and best length <=512. It might improve.
```
def sota_lp_step(p):
   from scipy.optimize import linprog (import at top)
   p=normalize(p); n=p.size
   c=fft_autocorr(p); M=float(c.max())
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
   for i in range(n):
      end = min(2*n-1, i+n)
      A[i:end, i] = p[:end-i]
   res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=[(0,1e6)]*n, method='highs')
   if success: g=res.x; gs=sum(g); if gs>0: g/=gs; return g
```
Then blend line search:
```
def sota_improve(p, budget):
   t_start=[1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.1,0.05]
   best=p; best_s=score_fast(p)
   g=sota_lp_step(p)
   for t in t_start:
      h=(1-t)*p + t*g; h=normalize(h); s=score_fast(h); if s<best_s: best=h...
   return best
```

```
def latent_basics(n):
  idx=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
  t=idx/(max(1,n-1))
  logL=np.log(idx+1.0)
  logR=np.log(n-idx+1.0)
  basis=[]
  basis.append(t)
  basis.append(t*t)
  for freq in (1,2,4,8,16):
      ang=2π*freq*t
      basis.append(np.sin(ang)); basis.append(np.cos(ang))
  basis.append(np.exp(-((t-0.5)/0.25)**2))
  basis.append(np.exp(-((t-0.3)/0.15)**2))
  basis.append(np.exp(-((t-0.7)/0.15)**2))
  basis=np.vstack(basis).astype(float)
  logL -= np.max(logL)? Not necessary; scale. But to avoid wide values, we can center logs? logL ~0..6.7; fine. Could subtract mean from basis to reduce redundancy with constant (removed by centering logit). We can center basis: `basis = basis - basis.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)`. Good.
  return logL, logR, basis
```
Number of basis: t,t² (2)+ freq 1,2,4,8,16 (10)+3 gauss=15. K=15. Params dim17. DE okay.

Should we include log left with `idx+0.5` to avoid log0? using idx+1. Good.

Let's write `score_fast`:
```
def score_fast(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size<128: c=np.convolve(a,a)
   else: c=fft_autocorr(a)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01: return inf
   m=c.max()
   val=2*a.size*m/(s*s)
   return val if finite else inf
```
For normalized p sum1. If a size 1, conv length1.

`fft_autocorr` for p normalized small; if `c` has tiny negative, max maybe slightly negative? Clip negative to 0 for max? But if all positives tiny, negative clipping could understate. Use `c = np.maximum(c,0.0)`. However true coefficients nonnegative, so clipping rounding safe. Returns real.

In L-BFGS, if M very small (n large), beta=scale/M huge; z=beta*(c-M) can under/overflow. Use `z = np.minimum(beta*(c-M), 0.0)`, clip lower -700. If beta > 1e6? M~1e-3, beta ~10M? For M=0.001, beta=10000; okay. If n=1024, M~0.0008, beta~12500. Good.

a=np.nan_to_num(a,...); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)

if s<=1e-12:
   n=max(1,a.size); return central_seq(n).astype(float)

`score_exact(a)`:
```
def score_exact(a):
 arr=... clip 0..1000
 if arr.size==0: return inf
 s=arr.sum()
 if s<0.01: return inf
 c=np.convolve(arr,arr)
 if c.size==0: return inf
 m=float(np.max(c))
 if not math.isfinite(m): return inf
 val=2*len(arr)*m/(s*s)
 return val if finite else inf
```
This matches evaluator except evaluator returns np.inf if invalid. Good.

`fft_autocorr(a)`:
```
def fft_autocorr(a):
 a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size; L=2*n-1
 N=1; while N<L: N*=2
 f=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
 c=np.fft.irfft(f*np.conj(f),N)[:L]
 c=np.real(c)
 c=np.maximum(c,0)
 return c
```
For real inputs, c nonnegative. If N small.

`smooth_obj_y(y,beta)`: No time. Use p from y; if p None return (1e100, zeros). In scipy minimize, y is ndarray. Need handle `beta` maybe array? float.
```
def smooth_obj_y(y, beta):
   m=int(np.size(y)); n=m+1
   y=np.asarray(y,float).ravel()
   terms=np.empty(n); terms[0]=0; terms[1:]=y
   mexp=np.max(terms); if not finite return ...
   z=np.exp(terms-mexp); s=z.sum(); if s<=0 return
   p=z/s
   c = np.convolve(p,p) if n<128 else fft_autocorr(p)
   M=float(c.max())
   if M<=0 or not finite: return ...
   beta=float(beta)
   if beta<=0: beta=1e6? # but M small, beta scale external
   zeta=beta*(c-M); zeta=np.clip(zeta,-700,0)
   ez=np.exp(zeta); Z=ez.sum(); if Z<=0: ...
   L=M+math.log(Z)/beta
   w=ez/Z
   # convolve
   if n<128: conv=np.convolve(p, w[::-1]) else: conv=fft_convolve(p,w[::-1])? We can use direct np.convolve because gradient often.
   conv=np.convolve(p,w[::-1])
   idx=2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
   h=conv[idx]
   gp=2*h
   G=float(np.dot(p,gp))
   grad=2*n*(gp[1:] - p[1:]*G)
   val=2*n*L
   if not finite: return 1e100,zeros
   grad=np.nan_to_num(...)
   return val,grad
```
Could use FFT for convolve to speed n=1024. Let's implement `fft_convolve(a,b)`:
```
def fft_convolve(a,b):
    L=len(a)+len(b)-1; N=1; while N<L: N*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)[:L]
```

`lbfgs_polish(p, deadline)`: returns p.
```
def lbfgs_polish(p, deadline):
   p0=normalize(p); n=p0.size
   best=p0; best_s=score_fast(p0)
   rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
   if minimize is None or n==1 or rem<2: return best
   y=init_y_from_p(best)
   if y.size==0: return best
   bounds=[(-50,50)]*y.size
   # total iterations estimate
   if n<=128: total=120 if rem>10 else 50
   elif n<=384: total=100 if rem>8 else 40
   elif n<=768: total=70 if rem>6 else 25
   else: total=50 if rem>5 else 18
   sizes=... sum<=total
   for stage,(scale,it) in enumerate([(10,sizes[0]),(20,sizes[1]),(40,sizes[2])]):
      rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
      if rem<1 or it<=0: break
      M=... max score from c
      if not finite: break
      beta=scale/max(M,1e-15)
      # Reduce maxiter by available time rough: per iter 0.005s? 
      maxit=int(max(5, min(it, max(5, rem*60 if n<=512 else rem*30))))
      try res=minimize(...)
      except: break
      y2=np.asarray(res.x,...); p2=p_from_y(y2); if p2 is None: continue
      # optionally sym
      s2=score_fast(p2)
      if finite and s2<best_s: best=p2; best_s=s2
   return best
```

`interpolate_log(a,n)`:
```
def interpolate_log(a,n):
 a=normalize(a)
 if a.size==n: return a
 if a.size==1: return np.full(n,1/n)
 oldx=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); newx=np.linspace(0,1,n)
 vals=np.log(a+1e-300)
 newv=np.exp(np.interp(newx, oldx, vals))
 newv=np.where(np.isfinite(newv),newv,0)
 s=newv.sum()
 if s<=1e-12: newv=central_seq(n)
 else: newv/=s
 return newv
```

Let's implement `sota_improve`:
```
def sota_improve(p, deadline):
   p=normalize(p); n=p.size
   if linprog is None or n<2 or n>512 or deadline-time.monotonic()<20: return p
   c=fft_autocorr(p); M=float(c.max())
   if not finite or M<=0: return p
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
   for i in range(n):
       end=min(2*n-1, i+n)
       A[i:end, i]=p[:end-i]
   try res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=[(0,1e6)]*n, method='highs')
   except: return p
   if not res.success or res.x is None: return p
   g=normalize(res.x)
   best=p; best_s=score_fast(p)
   for t in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.1,0.03):
       h=(1-t)*p + t*g; h=normalize(h)
       sh=score_fast(h)
       if finite and sh<best_s: best=h; best_s=sh
   return best
```
The LP may return p if g same. Blend t=1 if g better.

```python
"""
Optimize a nonnegative step sequence a to minimize
    2*len(a)*max(autocorr(a)) / (sum(a))**2.

This program combines:
  1. A custom differential-evolution search over a smooth latent representation
     (edge singular exponents plus Fourier / polynomial / Gaussian basis).
  2. Full-variable softmax L-BFGS-B polishing of the best latent candidates.
  3. A state-of-the-art style linear-programming blending step.
  4. Final low-frequency stochastic refinement and validation.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None

TARGET_UPPER_BOUND = 1.5027


def central_seq(n):
    """One-sided central-binomial-like coefficients, starting with 1."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(n - 1):
        a[i + 1] = a[i] * (i + 0.5) / (i + 1.0)
    return a


def normalize(a):
    """Strictly nonnegative finite normalized vector, with safe fallback."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = central_seq(64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return central_seq(a.size if a.size > 0 else 64).astype(np.float64)
    return a / s


def score_exact(a):
    """Authoritative-compatible score using direct convolution."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(arr, arr)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    val = float(2.0 * arr.size * m / (s * s))
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def fft_autocorr(a):
    """Fast linear autocorrelation, returning nonnegative coefficients."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if need <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    N = 1
    while N < need:
        N <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fa), N)[:need]
    c = np.real(c)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def fft_convolve(a, b):
    """Fast linear convolution for nonnegative real vectors."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(b.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = n + m - 1
    N = 1
    while N < need:
        N <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)[:need]
    c = np.real(c)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def score_fast(a):
    """Fast score for internal optimization; uses FFT for longer sequences."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return float("inf")
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    if arr.size < 128:
        c = np.convolve(arr, arr)
    else:
        c = fft_autocorr(arr)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    val = float(2.0 * arr.size * m / (s * s))
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def log_softmax(logit):
    """Stable softmax of a logit vector."""
    logit = np.asarray(logit, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if logit.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logit)):
        return None
    mx = float(np.max(logit))
    z = np.exp(logit - mx)
    s = float(z.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return z / s


def latent_basics(n):
    """Basis functions for smooth latent parametrization."""
    n = int(max(2, n))
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = idx / float(max(1, n - 1))
    logL = np.log(idx + 1.0)
    logR = np.log(float(n) - idx + 1.0)

    basis = []
    basis.append(t)
    basis.append(t * t)
    basis.append(np.sqrt(np.maximum(t, 1e-12)))
    basis.append(np.sqrt(np.maximum(1.0 - t, 1e-12)))

    for freq in (1, 2, 4, 8, 16):
        ang = 2.0 * math.pi * freq * t
        basis.append(np.sin(ang))
        basis.append(np.cos(ang))

    basis.append(np.exp(-((t - 0.5) / 0.25) ** 2))
    basis.append(np.exp(-((t - 0.28) / 0.16) ** 2))
    basis.append(np.exp(-((t - 0.72) / 0.16) ** 2))

    basis = np.vstack(basis).astype(np.float64)
    # Removing the mean of each basis is harmless because latent_objective
    # centeres the whole logit before softmax.
    basis = basis - basis.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    return logL, logR, basis


def latent_bounds(k_basis):
    """Bounds for [left_edge, right_edge, basis coefficients...]."""
    out = [(-1.0, 2.0), (-1.0, 2.0)]
    for _ in range(int(k_basis)):
        out.append((-5.0, 5.0))
    return out


def latent_objective(x, n, logL, logR, basis):
    """Score of the latent parametrization."""
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    basis = np.asarray(basis, dtype=np.float64)
    k = basis.shape[0]

    if x.size != 2 + k:
        return 10.0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 10.0

    rL = float(x[0])
    rR = float(x[1])
    coeff = x[2:]

    logit = -rL * logL.astype(np.float64) - rR * logR.astype(np.float64)
    if k:
        logit = logit + coeff.dot(basis)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logit)):
        return 10.0

    logit = logit - float(np.mean(logit))
    p = log_softmax(logit)
    if p is None:
        return 10.0
    val = score_fast(p)
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else 10.0


def make_latent_p(x, n, logL, logR, basis):
    """Convert latent parameters to a probability vector."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    basis = np.asarray(basis, dtype=np.float64)
    k = basis.shape[0]

    if x.size != 2 + k:
        return normalize(central_seq(n))

    rL = float(x[0])
    rR = float(x[1])
    coeff = x[2:]

    logit = -rL * logL.astype(np.float64) - rR * logR.astype(np.float64)
    if k:
        logit = logit + coeff.dot(basis)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logit)):
        return normalize(central_seq(n))

    logit = logit - float(np.mean(logit))
    p = log_softmax(logit)
    if p is None:
        return normalize(central_seq(n))
    return p


def sample_bounds(bounds, rng):
    """Uniform random point inside box bounds."""
    out = np.empty(len(bounds), dtype=np.float64)
    for i, (lo, hi) in enumerate(bounds):
        out[i] = float(rng.uniform(lo, hi))
    return out


def clip_params(x, bounds):
    """Clip a parameter vector to box bounds."""
    out = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if out.size != len(bounds):
        return out
    for i, (lo, hi) in enumerate(bounds):
        out[i] = float(min(max(out[i], lo), hi))
    return out


def pick_three(rng, size, avoid):
    """Pick three distinct indices different from avoid."""
    while True:
        a = int(rng.integers(0, size))
        b = int(rng.integers(0, size))
        c = int(rng.integers(0, size))
        if a == avoid or b == avoid or c == avoid:
            continue
        if a == b or b == c or a == c:
            continue
        return a, b, c


def latent_search(n, deadline, seed, budget_s):
    """Cheap custom DE over the latent basis."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    logL, logR, basis = latent_basics(n)
    dim = 2 + basis.shape[0]
    bounds = latent_bounds(basis.shape[0])

    size_pop = 26
    max_evals = int(max(500, min(8000, max(1.0, float(budget_s)) * 90.0)))

    pop = []
    fs = []

    central = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
    central[0] = 0.5
    pop.append(central)

    right = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
    right[1] = 0.5
    pop.append(right)

    uniform = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
    pop.append(uniform)

    # A few structured initial guesses.
    for _ in range(3):
        x = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
        x[0] = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 1.2))
        x[1] = float(rng.uniform(-0.2, 1.0))
        x[2:] = rng.normal(0.0, 1.5, x[2:].size)
        pop.append(clip_params(x, bounds))

    while len(pop) < size_pop:
        pop.append(clip_params(sample_bounds(bounds, rng), bounds))

    for x in pop:
        f = latent_objective(x, n, logL, logR, basis)
        fs.append(f)

    evals = len(fs)
    end_time = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(budget_s)))

    best_idx = int(np.argmin(fs))
    best_f = float(fs[best_idx])
    F = 0.75
    CR = 0.70
    stagnant = 0

    while evals < max_evals and time.monotonic() < end_time:
        for i in range(size_pop):
            if evals >= max_evals or time.monotonic() >= end_time:
                break

            v1, v2, v3 = pick_three(rng, size_pop, i)
            base = pop[i]
            mut = pop[v1] + F * (pop[v2] - pop[v3])
            trial = np.array(base, dtype=np.float64)
            jrand = int(rng.integers(0, dim))

            for j in range(dim):
                if (rng.random() < CR) or (j == jrand):
                    val = mut[j]
                else:
                    val = base[j]
                lo, hi = bounds[j]
                val = min(max(float(val), lo), hi)
                trial[j] = val

            ftrial = latent_objective(trial, n, logL, logR, basis)
            evals += 1

            if math.isfinite(ftrial) and ftrial <= fs[i] + 1e-12:
                pop[i] = trial
                fs[i] = ftrial

            if ftrial < best_f - 1e-12:
                best_f = ftrial
                best_idx = i
                stagnant = 0
            else:
                stagnant += 1

        if stagnant > 3 * size_pop:
            # Replace the worst individual(s) with fresh random restarts.
            order = sorted(range(size_pop), key=lambda i2: fs[i2])
            worst = order[-1]
            pop[worst] = clip_params(sample_bounds(bounds, rng), bounds)
            fs[worst] = latent_objective(pop[worst], n, logL, logR, basis)
            evals += 1
            stagnant = 0

    order = sorted(range(size_pop), key=lambda i2: fs[i2])
    top = []
    for i2 in order[:6]:
        top.append(pop[i2])
    return top


def interpolate_log(a, n):
    """Interpolate a sequence in log-space to a new length."""
    a = normalize(a)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == n:
        return a
    if a.size <= 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)

    oldx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    newx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    oldv = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    newv = np.exp(np.interp(newx, oldx, oldv))
    newv = np.asarray(newv, dtype=np.float64)
    newv = np.where(np.isfinite(newv), newv, 0.0)
    newv = np.maximum(newv, 0.0)
    s = float(newv.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return central_seq(n).astype(np.float64)
    return newv / s


def p_from_y(y):
    """Recover softmax probability from anchored log-ratios y[1:] (with y[0]=0)."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    y = np.clip(y, -50.0, 50.0)
    n = int(y.size) + 1
    terms = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    terms[0] = 0.0
    terms[1:] = y
    mx = float(np.max(terms))
    z = np.exp(terms - mx)
    s = float(z.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return z / s


def init_y_from_p(p):
    """Initial log-ratios from a probability vector."""
    p = normalize(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    p = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    p0 = float(p[0])
    if not math.isfinite(p0) or p0 < 1e-14:
        p[0] = 1e-10
        p = p / p.sum()
        p0 = float(p[0])

    rest = np.maximum(p[1:], 1e-300)
    y = np.log(rest / max(p0, 1e-300))
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    return np.clip(y, -45.0, 45.0)


def smooth_obj_y(y, beta):
    """Smooth softmax objective and gradient for the anchored softmax."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(y.size)
    n = m + 1

    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    y = np.clip(y, -50.0, 50.0)

    terms = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    terms[0] = 0.0
    terms[1:] = y
    mx = float(np.max(terms))
    if not math.isfinite(mx):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(m, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.exp(terms - mx)
    s = float(z.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(m, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    p = z / s

    if n < 128:
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
    else:
        c = fft_autocorr(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(m, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(m, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if not math.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0

    zeta = beta * (c - M)
    zeta = np.clip(zeta, -700.0, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(zeta)
    Z = float(ez.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(m, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    w = ez / Z
    L = M + math.log(Z) / beta

    if n < 256:
        conv = np.convolve(p, w[::-1])
    else:
        conv = fft_convolve(p, w[::-1])

    if conv.size < (3 * n - 2):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(m, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    idx = 2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n)
    h = np.asarray(conv[idx], dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    gp = 2.0 * h

    G = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    grad = 2.0 * float(n) * (gp[1:] - p[1:] * G)
    val = 2.0 * float(n) * L

    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(m, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(m, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    return float(val), grad


def lbfgs_polish(p, deadline):
    """Staged softmax L-BFGS-B polishing."""
    p0 = normalize(p)
    n = int(p0.size)
    best = p0.copy()
    best_s = score_fast(p0)

    if minimize is None or n <= 1:
        return best

    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
    if rem < 2.0:
        return best

    y = init_y_from_p(best)
    if y.size == 0:
        return best

    bounds = [(-50.0, 50.0)] * y.size

    if n <= 256:
        per_s = 60
    elif n <= 512:
        per_s = 28
    elif n <= 1024:
        per_s = 14
    else:
        per_s = 7

    # Rough total iteration budget, split into three smoothing stages.
    total = int(min(180, max(20, rem * per_s)))
    s0, s1, s2 = int(0.36 * total), int(0.34 * total), max(6, total - int(0.70 * total))
    stages = [(10.0, s0), (20.0, s1), (45.0, s2)]

    for scale, base_iter in stages:
        rem = float(deadline) - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 1.5:
            break
        c = fft_autocorr(best)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        beta = scale / max(M, 1e-15)

        maxit = int(max(4, min(base_iter, rem * per_s)))
        # Shrinking late can avoid overly long final line searches.
        if rem < 5.0:
            maxit = min(maxit, 12)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                fun=smooth_obj_y,
                x0=best and init_y_from_p(best) or y,
                args=(beta,),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxit,
                    "maxls": 40,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-4,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            y2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            break

        p2 = p_from_y(y2)
        if p2 is None or p2.size != n:
            continue

        # Try symmetrization as a cheap basin bridge.
        candidates = [p2, 0.5 * (p2 + p2[::-1])]
        for cand in candidates:
            cand = normalize(cand)
            sc = score_fast(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
                best = cand.copy()
                best_s = sc

    return best


def sota_lp_step(p):
    """One normalized SOTA LP direction: max 1^T g s.t. p*g <= M(p)."""
    p = normalize(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if linprog is None or n < 2 or n > 512:
        return None

    c = fft_autocorr(p)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        end = min(2 * n - 1, i + n)
        if end > i:
            A[i:end, i] = p[: end - i]

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c=-np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=[(0.0, 1e6)] * n,
            method="highs",
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if not getattr(res, "success", False) or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return g / s


def sota_improve(p, deadline):
    """Apply one LP move and line-search blends."""
    p = normalize(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_s = score_fast(p)
    rem = float(deadline) - time.monotonic()
    if rem < 15.0:
        return best

    g = sota_lp_step(p)
    if g is None:
        return best

    for t in (1.0, 0.80, 0.60, 0.40, 0.25, 0.12, 0.05):
        h = (1.0 - t) * p + t * g
        h = normalize(h)
        sh = score_fast(h)
        if math.isfinite(sh) and sh < best_s:
            best = h.copy()
            best_s = sh

        h2 = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
        h2 = normalize(h2)
        sh2 = score_fast(h2)
        if math.isfinite(sh2) and sh2 < best_s:
            best = h2.copy()
            best_s = sh2

    return best


def random_smooth_latent_p(n, logL, logR, basis, rng, alpha=1.5, central_bias=0.0):
    """Random smooth latent point converted to p."""
    dim = 2 + basis.shape[0]
    x = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
    x[0] = float(central_bias + rng.normal(0.3, 0.45))
    x[1] = float(central_bias + rng.normal(0.2, 0.35))
    if not all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.zeros(dim)
    x[0] = max(0.0, min(2.0, x[0]))
    x[1] = max(0.0, min(2.0, x[1]))
    if basis.shape[0]:
        x[2:] = rng.normal(0.0, alpha, basis.shape[0])

    bounds = latent_bounds(basis.shape[0])
    return make_latent_p(clip_params(x, bounds), n, logL, logR, basis)


def random_noisy_p(n, rng):
    """Random wide-smooth noisy candidate."""
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    logit = -0.55 * np.log(idx + 1.0)
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    # Low-pass by a few circular smoothings.
    if n > 8:
        w = np.ones(int(max(3, min(n, round(n / 32)))) + 1)
        for _ in range(3):
            noise = np.convolve(noise, w, mode="same")
            noise = noise / max(1.0, float(noise.sum()))
    logit += 1.25 * noise
    logit -= float(np.mean(logit))
    p = log_softmax(logit)
    if p is None:
        return normalize(central_seq(n))
    return p


def random_block_p(n, rng):
    """Two-level or block-shaped candidate."""
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    q = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.9))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    split = int(q * n)
    if split <= 0:
        split = 1
    elif split >= n:
        split = n - 1
    left_h = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 2.0))
    right_h = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 1.25))
    a[:split] = left_h
    a[split:] = right_h
    # optionally add a smooth hump
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        center = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        width = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.35))
        t = idx / float(max(1, n - 1))
        a += float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0)) * np.exp(-((t - center) / width) ** 2)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return normalize(a)


def anneal_polish(p, budget_s, rng):
    """Final stochastic refinement with low-frequency and block moves."""
    cur = normalize(p).copy()
    n = int(cur.size)
    best = cur.copy()
    cur_s = score_fast(cur)
    best_s = cur_s

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    step = 0
    no_improve = 0
    T = 0.012

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        step += 1
        move = int(rng.integers(0, 3))

        if move == 0:
            # Low-frequency logarithmic sinusoidal modulation.
            t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n - 1))
            ph1 = float(rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi))
            ph2 = float(rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi))
            a1 = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6))
            a2 = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6))
            move_logit = a1 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * t + ph1) + a2 * np.cos(4.0 * math.pi * t + ph2)
            amp = float(0.12 + 0.18 * rng.random())
            logp = np.log(np.maximum(cur, 1e-300))
            cand = log_softmax(logp + amp * move_logit)
        elif move == 1:
            # One or two multiplying blocks.
            cand = cur.copy()
            nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 3))
            for _ in range(nblocks):
                max_w = max(1, n // 16)
                width = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, max_w + 1)))
                width = max(1, min(n, width))
                start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
                sig = float(0.12 + 0.35 * rng.random())
                scale = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, width))
                scale = np.where(np.isfinite(scale), scale, 1.0)
                cand[start:start + width] *= scale
            cand = normalize(cand)
        else:
            # Coordinate-ish multiplicative wiggles.
            cand = cur.copy()
            k = int(rng.integers(3, max(4, min(n, 64))))
            idxs = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
            mult = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, k))
            cand[idxs] *= mult
            cand = normalize(cand)

        if cand is None:
            continue
        cand = normalize(cand)
        sc = score_fast(cand)

        if math.isfinite(sc):
            accept = False
            if sc <= cur_s:
                accept = True
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                if (sc - cur_s) < T and rng.random() < 0.25:
                    accept = True
                no_improve += 1

            if accept:
                cur = cand.copy()
                cur_s = sc
                if sc < best_s:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_s = sc

            if no_improve >= 160:
                cur = best.copy()
                cur_s = best_s
                no_improve = 0
                T = max(0.002, 0.7 * T)
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if step % 400 == 0:
            T = max(0.001, 0.96 * T)
            print(f"anneal step={step} n={n} best_fast={best_s:.10f}")

    return best, float(best_s)


def try_candidate(p, best_a, best_s):
    """Exact-score update with symmetrization check."""
    if p is None:
        return best_a, best_s
    cand = normalize(p)
    sc = score_exact(cand)
    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
        best_a, best_s = cand.copy(), sc

    if cand.size > 1:
        sym = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
        sym = normalize(sym)
        ss = score_exact(sym)
        if math.isfinite(ss) and ss < best_s:
            best_a, best_s = sym.copy(), ss

    return best_a, best_s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400):
    """Search for the best valid sequence within the time budget."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total - 2.5

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    # Safe global fallback: a long central sequence.
    best_a = normalize(central_seq(1024))
    best_s = score_exact(best_a)
    print(f"initial central best_score={best_s:.10f}, n={best_a.size}")

    # Use any externally supplied previous construction if present.
    try:
        hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if hs.size > 0:
            hs = normalize(hs)
            sh = score_exact(hs)
            if math.isfinite(sh):
                if sh < best_s:
                    best_a, best_s = hs.copy(), sh
                print(f"height_sequence_1 score={sh:.10f}, n={hs.size}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # ------------------- Phase 1: latent DE + full polish at moderate n -------------------
    n1 = 512
    if total < 220:
        n1 = 256

    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 45.0:
        de_budget = min(75.0, max(12.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.22))
        logL, logR, basis = latent_basics(n1)
        try:
            top_latent = latent_search(n1, deadline, seed, de_budget)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("latent search failed:", exc)
            top_latent = []

        starts = []

        for x in top_latent[:4]:
            p = make_latent_p(x, n1, logL, logR, basis)
            starts.append(p)

        starts.append(normalize(central_seq(n1)))

        try:
            hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if hs.size > 0:
                starts.append(interpolate_log(hs, n1))
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Add unique broad smooth and structural random starts.
        for alpha in (0.8, 1.5, 2.2):
            starts.append(random_smooth_latent_p(n1, logL, logR, basis, rng, alpha=alpha))
        for _ in range(2):
            starts.append(random_noisy_p(n1, rng))
        for _ in range(2):
            starts.append(random_block_p(n1, rng))

        scored = []
        for p in starts:
            p = normalize(p)
            if p.size != n1:
                p = interpolate_log(p, n1)
            sc = score_fast(p)
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, p))

        scored.sort()

        max_starts = 4
        if deadline - time.monotonic() < 90.0:
            max_starts = 3
        if deadline - time.monotonic() < 45.0:
            max_starts = 2

        for idx in range(min(max_starts, len(scored))):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            sc0, p0 = scored[idx]
            if idx > 0 and sc0 > best_s + 0.45:
                continue

            print(f"polishing latent start {idx} n={n1} fast_score={sc0:.10f}")
            p_pol = lbfgs_polish(p0, deadline)
            best_a, best_s = try_candidate(p_pol, best_a, best_s)
            print(f"after polish n={p_pol.size} exact_best={best_s:.10f}")

        # One SOTA LP move on the current best if it is modest-length.
        if (
            best_s > TARGET_UPPER_BOUND
            and deadline - time.monotonic() > 35.0
            and (best_a.size <= 512 or n1 <= 512)
        ):
            try:
                p_s = interpolate_log(best_a, min(512, max(128, best_a.size)))
                p_s = lbfgs_polish(p_s, deadline)
                p_s = sota_improve(p_s, deadline)
                best_a, best_s = try_candidate(p_s, best_a, best_s)
                print(f"after SOTA length={p_s.size} exact_best={best_s:.10f}")
            except Exception as exc:
                print("SOTA skipped:", exc)

    # ------------------- Phase 2: upscale and polish larger lengths -------------------
    target_lengths = []
    if best_a.size < 768:
        target_lengths.append(768)
    if best_a.size < 1024:
        target_lengths.append(1024)

    for n2 in target_lengths:
        if deadline - time.monotonic() < 20.0:
            break
        p0 = interpolate_log(best_a, n2)
        p1 = lbfgs_polish(p0, deadline)
        best_a, best_s = try_candidate(p1, best_a, best_s)
        print(f"upscaled n={n2} exact_best={best_s:.10f}")

    # If best remained short, try a long polish from a very long central seed too.
    if best_a.size < 1024 and deadline - time.monotonic() > 30.0:
        p_long = normalize(central_seq(1024))
        p_long = lbfgs_polish(p_long, deadline)
        best_a, best_s = try_candidate(p_long, best_a, best_s)
        print(f"long central polish n={p_long.size} exact_best={best_s:.10f}")

    # ------------------- Phase 3: final stochastic refinement -------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 15.0:
        anneal_budget = min(40.0, rem - 4.0)
        if best_a.size <= 384:
            anneal_budget = min(50.0, anneal_budget)
        try:
            p_ann, _ = anneal_polish(best_a, anneal_budget, rng)
            best_a, best_s = try_candidate(p_ann, best_a, best_s)
            print(f"after anneal n={p_ann.size} exact_best={best_s:.10f}")
        except Exception as exc:
            print("anneal skipped:", exc)

    # ------------------- Final validation and safe scaling -------------------
    best_a = normalize(best_a)
    final_s = score_exact(best_a)
    if not math.isfinite(final_s):
        best_a = normalize(central_seq(1024))
        final_s = score_exact(best_a)

    # Scale to the conventional normalization sum=sqrt(2n). Score is invariant.
    target_sum = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * float(best_a.size)))
    s_sum = float(best_a.sum())
    if math.isfinite(s_sum) and s_sum > 0.0:
        best_a = best_a * (target_sum / s_sum)

    # Authoritative evaluator is already imported in the environment.
    try:
        ext = float(evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in best_a]))
        if math.isfinite(ext):
            final_s = min(final_s, ext)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"FINAL best_score={final_s:.10f}, n={best_a.size}")

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)) or best_a.size == 0 or float(np.sum(best_a)) < 0.01:
        best_a = normalize(central_seq(1024))

    return [float(v) for v in best_a]
```
